data-option=\"0\">A. A foundation spokesman called it a performance staged by people with a financial interest in scandal. The theatre manager read the message, looked at the unpaid repair bill for the stage door, and asked where the financial interest was hiding.","June placed the supplier's invoice on the table. \"We can start with a cable. It's less photogenic than a feud.\"","\"An inexcusable choice,\" Rowan said. \"I dressed for a feud.\"","He wore a dark suit and a tie so beautiful that Mara suspected it had its own legal adviser. Cassian stood behind her, fastening the repaired pin at her shoulder. His fingers brushed the bare skin beneath her hair.","\"Hold still,\" he murmured.","\"You are making that difficult.\"","His smile was visible in the mirror. The pin settled into place, and he kissed the back of her neck once before stepping away.","Mara forgot the spokesman entirely.","Then Ivo dropped a crate beside the door and brought them back to the world.","The room filled with technicians, a representative of the Water Authority, two independent researchers, and residents from the hospital district. Els arrived with the fisherman whose net had been empty. Peta sat near the exit. She had received notice that her employment was under review. The notice did not prevent her from putting her own emails on the table.","The briefing was not a trial. It had a smaller, more difficult aim: allow questions to be asked while the original sources remained available to people qualified to check them.","Ivo began with a stand-alone contact station. No one sat in its chair. A transparent cover enclosed the controls.","\"This one is disconnected from the clinical network,\" he said. \"We're using a simulated contact signal, not a patient.\"","He sent a scheduling command from a separate terminal. The command appeared at the station and stopped. The display asked for local contact from a paired node.","The Water Authority representative leaned forward. \"So a remote message reaches it.\"","\"Yes. The message and the session are different stages.\"","Ivo enabled the test load. The station registered the contact condition. An indicator changed colour. He disabled it, and the station stopped again.","A researcher asked whether that demonstrated the biological effect. Noor shook her head.","\"It demonstrates the gating we can inspect here. The clinical claim requires the session records, patient accounts, and independent examination. Those are being reviewed separately. We are not going to repeat a harmful procedure to make an attractive presentation.\"","The room remained quiet. The refusal was more unsettling than a dramatic display would have been.","Mara showed the physical cable route next: monitoring unit, return circuit, communications rack, contact station. Her photographs could be compared with Dorian's work record and Peta's installation plan. The spiral supplier mark appeared in each.","Then Els put the water results beside the valve photographs.","The fisherman stood. He did not speak about neurobiology or networks. He gave the dates on his calendar and described what he had seen at the inlet. He did not call every dead fish a victim of the same cause. He asked why his early complaints had been answered by a film crew instead of a sampling team.","Nobody had a good reply.","By evening, the foundation had invited a smaller group to the conservatory. Selene would address the concern personally, its message said, and correct the comparison being made between an environmental project and a clinical programme.","\"A transparent attempt to change the scenery,\" June said.","\"Then we go,\" Mara replied. \"And take the original scenery with us.\"","They arrived as the sun struck the glass roof. The public pool shone beneath a suspended globe. A dove sat on the outer frame, watching donors mingle under a model of a forest canopy. Mara wondered what it would make of a wildlife refuge that locked its human inhabitants below ground.","Selene greeted her with a smile designed for the cameras.","\"I'm pleased we can have a constructive conversation.\"","\"So am I. I brought the people who did the work.\"","Behind Mara, the fisherman removed his cap. Peta stood beside Els. Dorian carried his work photograph in a clear sleeve. The Water Authority representative had accepted the invitation on his own terms.","For one second, Selene's expression became quite still.","Mara enjoyed that second more than she should have.","Inside, a projection showed the greenhouse's contribution to the city's future. Selene spoke about innovation, scarcity, and the need to take risks. Private funding, she said, could achieve what cautious public institutions were too slow to attempt.","Mara waited until the slide showed a bright green public pipe.","\"Is that the research return?\" she asked.","\"It is a simplified diagram.\"","\"Then let's use the full one.\"","The representative from the Water Authority stepped forward with the preserved installation drawing. Selene looked towards Harrow. He moved to the projector, but June had already handed a printed copy to the chair of the funding panel.","The full drawing showed the cross-connection. Dorian's photograph showed the warning not to use it. The maintenance record showed a later change. The independent sample report showed research carriers outside the closed trial circuit.","No single person had to become the sole keeper of the story. The documents moved from hand to hand.","\"These are matters for specialists,\" Selene said.","\"They're here,\" Mara replied.","The words were quiet. Their effect was not.","Selene's gaze moved across Peta, Els, the technician, and the authority's representative. For once, expertise was not a person she could place behind a lectern and thank before they spoke.","Harrow said the connection had been temporary.","\"Then you have a date for closing it,\" Peta said. \"My warning email is six weeks old.\"","He opened his mouth, closed it, and asked to examine the document privately.","\"You can examine this copy here,\" June said.","Cassian stood beside Mara, not between her and the room. She could feel his warmth at her shoulder. She had once wanted a title powerful enough to silence people who could buy her name. Now she wanted the people with the original records to keep talking.","The funding chair asked Selene whether the clinical units used the same communications contractor. Selene referred to confidentiality. Noor answered before the word could become another closed door.","\"We are asking about equipment and authorization. No patient's private memory needs to be shown to establish who ordered a controller.\"","A small murmur moved through the guests.","Mara thought of her mother asleep beside a blue cup. She took the flower from beneath her gown and set it on the table, closed.","\"This led me to my mother,\" she said. \"It does not give anyone permission to turn her into a demonstration.\"","Selene looked at the silver petals. data-reset=\"58\">清空本章作答，重新自测</button><div class=\"question\" id=\"q-59-1\" data-question=\"58,0\"><p class=\"meta\">1. argument</p><p><strong>What does Mara’s reply to Harrow’s auction photograph chiefly accomplish?</strong></p><div class=\"options\"><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"0\">A. It proves that the ownership dispute has already been resolved.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"1\">B. It separates her disputed conduct from the independently checkable sources under discussion.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"2\">C. It shows that the photograph was made by the supplier.</button><button type=\"button\" data-option=\"3\">D. It makes her conduct irrelevant to any future hearing.</button></div><div class=\"feedback\" hidden=\"hidden\"></div></div></section><nav class=\"chapter-nav\" aria-label=\"Chapter navigation\"><button data-go=\"57\">← Previous</button><button data-go=\"59\">Next →</button></nav></article>"},{"id":"v05c12","title":"The Price of a Name","volume":"卷五｜城市下的花园","anchor":"chapter-0060","paragraphs":["Selene did not confess. She called for an orderly review, offered cooperation, and said that any unauthorized connection would be investigated. Her composure survived the evening. The water diagram did not survive unchanged.","By nine, the Water Authority had arranged an independent inspection of the cross-connection. The funding panel requested preservation of the installation files and suspended one pending payment while it examined the contract. No one ordered the whole system seized on the strength of Mara's photographs.","That was enough to make Harrow stop smiling.","Mara would have liked a triumph with cleaner edges. Instead, she had a tired technician being thanked, a fisherman being asked for his calendar, and Peta standing beside a donor who finally seemed interested in hearing what her work involved.","The results were small enough to be real.","Cassian found her on the conservatory steps with her shoes in one hand. The night breeze moved through the silver thread of her gown.","\"A thief on a pedestal,\" he said.","\"A temporary arrangement. The pedestal is uncomfortable.\"","He sat beside her and loosened his tie. She leaned against his shoulder.","\"You were magnificent,\" he said.","\"I was angry.\"","\"You can be both.\"","She turned towards him. The light from the glass roof caught his face, the familiar scar, the tired fondness he no longer tried to hide. She kissed him before the next photographer could make a decision about the angle.","June, coming down the steps with Rowan, sighed. \"I could sell those photographs and subsidize the hospital.\"","\"You could also enjoy another career,\" Mara said.","\"The hatred of an artist by her subjects. A historic tragedy.\"","Rowan put his coat around June's shoulders and steered her towards the car. \"Come along. We can critique them somewhere warm.\"","At Mercy House, Elena was asleep. The blue cup stood beside the bed. The nurse had written her name clearly on the door, with the agreed contact person underneath.","Mara stopped to look at it.","Elena Vale.","A name did not repair five years. It could prevent tomorrow from beginning with forty-three.","Independent records had begun to arrive: workshop photographs, the old repair register, Peta's notes about the anonymous patient, and a hospital transfer signature that did not match the supposed author. A former neighbour had identified Elena in a recent photograph. Noor had arranged further assessment without allowing the review to consume every waking hour.","The woman in the bed remained tired, sometimes confused, and capable of being irritated by bland food. Mara found the irritation almost reassuring.","She sat for a while, listening to her mother breathe. Cassian waited near the window. He had learned to give grief room without taking his presence away.","When Elena woke, she asked whether the meeting had gone well.","\"Some people will have to answer questions,\" Mara said.","\"Good. I was very bored of being the only one.\"","Mara laughed softly.","\"Can I have the flower?\" Elena asked.","She held it for a minute, then passed it back. \"Not to work. Just to look.\"","\"I didn't say anything.\"","\"You had the head nurse's expression.\"","Mara laughed. \"I've grown into authority.\"","\"A worrying development. Another day, then. I'm tired.\"","Mara closed the flower. \"Another day.\"","It was not a refusal to love her. It was a limit a living person could set. Mara accepted it, though the question remained like an unopened drawer.","In the corridor, Ivo and Noor were finishing a summary for the independent investigators. The distinction at its centre was simple enough to survive the rest of the terminology.","A command could reach a scheduled terminal. It could not, by itself, reach a person walking down the street. A session required a paired node, physical contact, and the other conditions recorded in the clinical protocol. The affected material was a limited recent interval at the time of use. Repeated sessions could leave older lasting gaps because those intervals had once been recent, not because the machine could now roam through childhood at will.","The carrier circuit helped deliver and calibrate the experimental system. Its connection to public supply required urgent environmental and health work. It was not a citywide remote-control channel hidden in every glass of water.","\"Elena and Ewan are disconnected,\" Noor said. \"That has to remain clear in anything we publish. So does the fact that other enrolled patients may still be connected elsewhere.\"","\"And logs aren't people,\" Ivo added. \"A data wipe and a new memory-suppression session are different dangers. Either could hide what happened. They don't do it in the same way.\"","Cassian checked the summary. Mara rested her hand on his arm. The machine had not been defeated. It had become an object with requirements, operators, and points where people could refuse to help it.","That mattered.","The next morning, the hospital garden smelled of wet earth and tea. Mara pushed Elena's chair into the courtyard while a nurse walked beside them. The roses had opened overnight.","Her mother looked at the pink heads, then at the dark soil beneath them. \"Someone has been feeding them too much.\"","\"We've found her,\" Mara told the nurse. \"A complaint about the gardening.\"","Elena smiled. A small expression, clear and exhausted. Mara would not have traded it for any flawless portrait of recovery.","Pip came down the path carrying a paper boat. He gave it to Elena with the solemnity of a captain appointing a passenger.","\"It's waterproof,\" he said. \"Mostly.\"","\"Then we won't take it into a typhoon.\"","He seemed satisfied by this rational precaution. He stayed to explain the fountain's current while Elena listened, her hand resting on the boat's folded sail.","Across the courtyard, a carpenter had begun replacing the damaged window frame. He had worked at the theatre before its delayed grant arrived. Now the hospital could pay him for the job instead of asking for another favor.","He raised a hand to Mara. \"No broken glass this time.\"","\"A disappointing lack of excitement.\"","\"I'm getting old. I prefer a bill I can collect.\"","Inside, the bookkeeper had pinned the next month's funding schedule beside the kitchen door. Its figures were modest, its spare margin painfully narrow. It did not promise that every resource problem had vanished. It showed staff when they would be paid and residents whom to ask if a charge looked wrong.","Mara stopped before it. She remembered arriving with a stolen flower and no idea how to keep a whole building warm. The place had not become wealthy. It had become less dependent on an unnamed benefactor's good mood.","Sister Agnes caught her looking.","\"You may admire the paperwork after you've carried the clean cups outside.\"","Mara obeyed. At the door she nearly collided with Peta, who had come to visit Elena with a small potted plant.","\"Not a research specimen,\" Peta said quickly. \"I grew it in my kitchen.\"","\"Can it survive my mother?\"","\"I've been warned she has opinions about watering.\"","They shared a smile. Peta's employment review remained unresolved, but her evidence had reached people beyond the company. She had an independent adviser and several professional witnesses. Mara could not give her back an easy career. She could make sure she no longer stood alone beside her warning.","Mara went to the kitchen for more tea. Cassian intercepted her beside the old stairwell.","\"I have an afternoon,\" he said.","\"A whole one?\"","\"No scheduled inspections. No dinner invitations. I thought you might steal it.\"","She looked towards the garden. \"She'll need me.\"","\"Agnes is here. Noor is here. And your mother asked me to get you out of the corridor before you wore a trench in it.\"","Mara narrowed her eyes. \"A conspiracy.\"","\"A small, mutually beneficial one.\"","She set down the teapot and took his hand.","They walked to the river. Nobody recognised them for the first ten minutes. Mara bought two cheap buns and made Cassian sit on a low wall to eat his. A smear of sugar caught at the corner of his mouth. She removed it with her thumb, then kissed the same spot because his expression invited worse behaviour.","\"You have no respect for my dignity,\" he said.","\"I tried. It was an inferior pastime.\"","He pulled her between his knees. She rested her hands on his shoulders, laughing as he looked up at her.","For an hour they had nothing to prove. The river moved below them, carrying light past the city that had tried to turn both of them into useful lies.","At dusk, they returned to Cassian's house. Mrs. Wren met them in the hall holding a thick envelope.","\"A court messenger,\" she said. \"He insisted on leaving it with someone who could confirm the address.\"","Mara read the front. Her full name stood beside Cassian's. The papers required attendance at a preliminary hearing about the disputed flower, the clinical records, and the removal of a patient from a private facility. A request for restrictions on further disclosure was attached.","She turned the page. The date was close enough to make the next week contract around it.","Cassian read over her shoulder. Neither spoke for a moment.","Then Mara placed the summons on the hall table.","\"Tomorrow,\" she said.","He looked at her. \"Tomorrow.\"","Upstairs, there was a clean bed. We are becoming efficient.\"","She opened one eye. He was sitting beside her in a clean shirt, with his hair still damp and a button fastened through the wrong hole. The sight made the word summons briefly less important. She corrected the button and drew him down for another kiss before the world could insist on being admitted.","A knock came at the door. Mrs. Wren said breakfast was ready, the messenger was waiting, and Admiral had taken a strong interest in the messenger's leather case. Cassian closed his eyes.","\"Your dog understands preservation of evidence,\" Mara said.","\"My dog understands ownership rather differently.\"","The documents concerned the greenhouse investigation, the equipment records, and Mara's retrieval of the silver flower at the original auction. A public preliminary hearing would examine the handling of material and competing claims about what had been taken. Her counsel had marked the date in blue. Cassian's accompanying notice required him to produce relevant official records.","Mara read the first paragraph twice. Then her phone began to ring.","June's message arrived before she could answer: DO NOT OPEN THE FRONT DOOR. The second message contained a headline.","VALE-ARDEN HEIRESS REMOVED FROM FAMILY REGISTER.","Beneath it was a photograph of Mara leaving the academy with her medal. Beside her face, somebody had placed a smaller picture of a prison gate. The caption promised an investigation into the \"birthright behind the hospital campaign.\"","For a moment she stared, too astonished to be angry. \"They have abolished a person I have been trying not to become.\"","Cassian took the phone. A family-history agency had withdrawn an unverified entry it had supplied to charity publicity lists. The name had been used in promotional material despite Mara's earlier correction. The agency's statement described the removal as a routine review. A spokesman quoted beneath it described her as a young woman whose ambition had outrun the facts.","\"I never asked them to put me there,\" she said.","\"We have the corrections.\"","\"Then we shall need larger letters.\"","The anger arrived at last, hot and useful. She wanted to call the agency, shake its executive director, and ask whether his latest installment of nonsense came with a money-back promise. Instead, she asked June what was happening at Mercy House.","A frenzied group of reporters had gathered near the gate. Some were filming the old sign; one had tried to ask a resident whether Mara's mother was genuinely her mother. Sister Agnes had closed the doors. Elena had not yet been shown the coverage.","Mara put down her toast.","\"Eat it,\" Cassian said.","\"I'm not hungry.\"","He picked up the plate. \"Then take it. I have learned that the interval between 'not hungry' and 'liable to bite someone' is alarmingly short.\"","She took the toast and her coat. He followed, carrying the case of papers. There was no elaborate promise to protect her from every consequence. He had simply understood where she needed to go.","June called again as they left. Her newsroom had received a proposed article describing Mara as the creation of a \"revolutionary social cure,\" a phrase supplied by a multinational publicity agency. A politician wanted to build on the story by promising to abolish charitable privileges. Nobody had asked which privileges Mara actually held.","\"The editor is tempted,\" June said. \"It has a villain, a groom, and no difficult budget.\"","\"Can you ascertain who supplied the approval page?\"","\"I'm trying. The agency spokesman says an attachment is missing. He also says a nuisance-lawsuit may follow if we misrepresent him.\"","Mara looked down at the summons. \"A bold emphasis on ethics.\"","\"I thought so. I'll ask him to send the whole packet. He can either fulfill his threat or help us print an accurate paragraph.\"","For the first time that morning, Mara smiled. She could count on June to find the useful question beneath an avalanche of elegant hostility.","The road to Mercy House ran beside the canal. Morning light made the water shine, while the mass media made a useful endeavour look like a confidence trick. Mara watched a cyclist juggle a bag of bread and a newspaper at the intersection. A month earlier, nobody would have recognized her. Now a photograph could turn her into an heiress, a fraud, or a social cure according to the publication buying it.","At the hospital gate, the cameras swung towards their car. Cassian's hand covered hers for one quiet second. \"Which door?\"","Mara looked at the crowded entrance. \"The front.\"","A reporter began before she had stepped onto the pavement. Had she deceived donors? Was the duke still supporting her? Had she invented a noble connection to obtain access to patients?","\"Good morning,\" she said. \"Move away from the ambulance entrance.\"","The reporter tried another question.","Mara pointed to the chalk line on the pavement. \"That space is for patients. If you want a statement, you can hear one from the other side.\"","June appeared at the edge of the mob, brisk and unsmiling. She directed the cameras back. Cassian opened the gate for a woman accompanying a man with a walking frame, then stood where no microphone could strike them as they passed.","Once the entrance was clear, Mara faced the lenses.","\"My name is Mara Vale. I have never claimed to be a Vale-Arden heiress. I corrected that description when it first appeared, and the correction is available to every newsroom asking today. My work at Mercy House rests on a written authorization, not a family-history entry.\"","\"Did you take the flower?\" someone called.","The question hit a different place. For weeks the flower had been a key, a record, a memory of Elena, and the beginning of everything. None of that changed the way she had obtained it.","\"I took it from the auction without permission,\" Mara said. \"I believed it contained my mother's work and property. The ownership dispute and my actions will be addressed through counsel. I will not describe taking it as something else.\"","The crowd stirred. A man asked whether she was admitting theft. Another wanted her to repeat the first sentence alone. June stepped forward, saying she had recorded the full statement and would publish it without removing the surrounding answer.","Mara continued. \"You may ask about my conduct. You may not make patients answer for it. Their treatment and privacy are not part of a publicity contest.\"","Cassian spoke only when a microphone followed her through the gate. \"You heard her.\"","The words were mild. The reporter stopped as though he had met a wall.","In the hall, a small crowd had gathered around the noticeboard. The latest paper was pinned beside the kitchen menu. Somebody had drawn a tail on the photograph of the charity spokesman. Gideon claimed ignorance with the solemnity of a man still holding the pencil.","\"Is this helping?\" Mara asked.","\"The likeness has improved.\"","A resident moved forward with a form she had been afraid to post. She wanted to know whether the hospital's assistance scheme would disappear if the newspapers kept shouting. Mara looked at the ordinary request, its careful handwriting and crossed-out date. The headline had reached further than her own embarrassment.","\"Your application goes in today,\" she said. \"Ada will help you find the right person. You aren't required to wait for people to stop talking about me.\"","The woman nodded. Mara turned the newspaper over before continuing down the hall.","Inside, Mara found Elena in a chair beside the window, wrapped in a blue blanket. Noor was helping her choose between two bowls of soup. Elena had selected neither because she wanted bread first and thought everybody was being needlessly solemn about it.","\"I brought toast,\" Mara said.","\"Then you have arrived with the essential qualification.\"","Her mother's voice still caught her unprepared. Recovery had not restored every missing interval, and some days concentration left Elena quickly. This morning she was watching the doorway with the old alertness. She noticed the toast, the papers, and the way Mara was holding her shoulders.","\"What's happened?\"","Mara sat on the bed's edge. She gave the accurate, manageable version. A false name had been withdrawn. Some people were using it to attack the hospital work. The flower would be considered at a hearing.","Elena's fingers closed around the blanket. \"I remember working on the hinge. I don't remember how it left my workshop.\"","\"You don't have to produce an answer because I need one.\"","\"Annoying child. I would prefer to produce it because I am curious.\"","Mara laughed. The sound shook on its way out, and Elena noticed that too. She reached for her daughter's hand. Her grip was not strong, but it was familiar.","Cassian set the document case beside the chair. Elena looked him over, including the button Mara had repaired.","\"You feed her?\" she asked.","\"When permitted.\"","\"An important basis for an alliance. She becomes unreasonable without lunch.\"","\"I was explaining that earlier.\"","\"I am in the room,\" Mara said.","\"Yes,\" Elena replied. \"It is a considerable improvement.\"","The words silenced them all for a moment. Mara held her mother's hand more tightly, then loosened her fingers before the tenderness became a demand. Elena needed rest, not a celebration every time she managed breakfast.","Noor drew the curtain halfway, taking the cameras outside out of sight. The hearing could wait long enough for soup. When Mara finally opened the case, she found an attachment she had overlooked: a glossy publicity page announcing the restoration of the \"lost Vale-Arden line.\"","At the bottom was a photograph of her silver flower. Beside it, beneath a forged-looking flourish, were the words APPROVED BY CLAIMANT.","Mara had never seen the page before.","She looked at Cassian. \"I think I know what we're looking for
